            $(document).ready(function () {

                let avdText = "";

                let initIndexBannerSlider = (indexBannerSpeed) => {


                    let indexBannerPaginationSlider = new Swiper('.pagination .swiper-container', {
                        slidesPerView: 'auto',
                        watchSlidesVisibility:true,
                        spaceBetween: 7,
                        breakpoints: {
                            768: {
                                spaceBetween: 80,
                                slidesPerView: '4',
                            }
                        }
                    });

                    let indexBannerOffersSlider = new Swiper('[data-banner="offers"]', {
                        loop: 1,
                        allowTouchMove: false,
                        effect: 'fade',
                        autoHeight: false,
                        fadeEffect: {
                            crossFade: true
                        },
                                                    on: {
                                slideChange: function (swiper) {

                                    avdText = $('[data-adv-text_'+swiper.realIndex+']').data('adv-text_' + swiper.realIndex);
                                    $('.text-bottom').text(avdText);

                                    /*if(swiper.realIndex == 2){
                                        $('.text-bottom').text('Реклама. Рекламодатель ООО "Продобро"');
                                    }else{
                                        $('.text-bottom').text('Реклама. Рекламодатель ООО "Курорт Доброград"');
                                    }*/
                                },
                            }
                                            });

                    let indexBannerSlider = new Swiper('[data-banner="main"]', {
                        loop: true,
                        preloadImages: false,
                        lazy: {
                            loadPrevNext: true,
                        },
                        effect: 'fade',
                        fadeEffect: {
                            crossFade: true
                        },
                        watchSlidesVisibility: true,
                        watchOverflow: true,
                        hashNavigation: false,
                        autoplay: {
                            delay: indexBannerSpeed,
                            disableOnInteraction: false
                        },
                        thumbs: {
                            autoScrollOffset: 1,
                            swiper: indexBannerPaginationSlider,
                        },
                        on: {
                            slideChange: function (swiper) {
                                indexBannerOffersSlider.slideTo(swiper.activeIndex);
                                if(swiper.realIndex == 0) {

                                    avdText = $('[data-adv-text_'+swiper.realIndex+']').data('adv-text_' + swiper.realIndex);
                                    $('.text-bottom').text(avdText);

                                    // console.log(swiper.autoplay);
                                    // console.log(swiper.realIndex);
                                    swiper.autoplay.delay = 60000;
                                }
                            },
                        }
                    });

                };


                 let timer = 5000;
                 if(window.location.pathname == '/banket/'){
                     timer = 5500;
				 }
                initIndexBannerSlider(timer);

            });

<script>
            (function() {
                // Предотвращаем повторную инициализацию
                if (window.SmartCaptchaModuleInitialized) return;
                window.SmartCaptchaModuleInitialized = true;

                const CONFIG = {
                    selectors: {
                        form: "form[data-smartcaptcha=\"true\"]",
                        submitButton: '[type="submit"], button:not([type]), input[type="image"]',
                        captchaContainer: '.smartcaptcha-container',
                        smartToken: '[name="smart-token"]'
                    },
                    exclusions: ["form[name=\"form_auth\"]","form[name=\"system_auth_form\"]","form[data-captcha-ignore]",".bx-auth-form",".search-form","form[action*=\"auth\"]","form[action*=\"login\"]","form[action*=\"search\"]"],
                    scriptUrl: "https://smartcaptcha.yandexcloud.net/captcha.js",
                    attributes: {
                        initialized: 'data-captcha-initialized',
                        ignore: 'data-captcha-ignore',
                        formId: 'data-captcha-form-id'
                    },
                    classes: {
                        captchaContainer: 'smartcaptcha-container'
                    },
                    timing: {
                        initDelay: 100,
                        domObserverDelay: 100,
                        scriptLoadRetry: 100
                    },
                    messages: {
                        captchaRequired: "Проверка SmartCaptcha не пройдена"                    }
                };

                // Хранилище инстансов капчи
                window.smartCaptchaInstances = window.smartCaptchaInstances || new Map();
                const captchaHtml = "\u003Cdiv class=\u0022smart-captcha\u0022 data-sitekey=\u0022ysc1_kzFuoIRokMSuCmiJTsaebjb39M5NmBrkUrdgIQhv1672f1cc\u0022 data-form-id=\u0022\u0022 data-invisible=\u0022true\u0022 data-hide-shield=\u0022true\u0022 data-shield-position=\u0022bottom-right\u0022\u003E\u003C\/div\u003E";

                /**
                 * Проверяет, подходит ли форма под исключения
                 */
                function isExcluded(form) {
                    if (form.hasAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.ignore)) {
                        return true;
                    }

                    for (const selector of CONFIG.exclusions) {
                        try {
                            if (form.matches(selector)) {
                                return true;
                            }
                        } catch (e) {
                            console.warn('[SmartCaptcha] Invalid exclusion selector:', selector);
                        }
                    }

                    return false;
                }

                /**
                 * Находит формы по селектору с учётом исключений
                 */
                function findForms() {
                    const forms = [];
                    try {
                        document.querySelectorAll(CONFIG.selectors.form).forEach(form => {
                            if (!isExcluded(form) && !form.hasAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.initialized)) {
                                forms.push(form);
                            }
                        });
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.error('[SmartCaptcha] Invalid form selector:', CONFIG.selectors.form);
                    }
                    return forms;
                }

                /**
                 * Загрузка скрипта SmartCaptcha
                 */
                function loadScript() {
                    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                        // Уже загружен
                        if (window.smartCaptcha?.render) {
                            resolve();
                            return;
                        }

                        // Скрипт уже добавлен на страницу
                        const existingScript = document.querySelector(`script[src*="smartcaptcha.yandexcloud.net"]`);
                        if (existingScript) {
                            // Ждём загрузки
                            if (window.smartCaptcha?.render) {
                                resolve();
                            } else {
                                existingScript.addEventListener('load', resolve);
                                existingScript.addEventListener('error', () => reject(new Error('Failed to load SmartCaptcha')));
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        const script = document.createElement('script');
                        script.src = CONFIG.scriptUrl;
                        script.defer = true;
                        script.onload = resolve;
                        script.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to load SmartCaptcha script'));
                        document.head.appendChild(script);
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * Ожидание инициализации SmartCaptcha
                 */
                async function waitForSmartCaptcha(maxAttempts = 50) {
                    let attempts = 0;
                    while (!window.smartCaptcha?.render && attempts < maxAttempts) {
                        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, CONFIG.timing.scriptLoadRetry));
                        attempts++;
                    }
                    if (!window.smartCaptcha?.render) {
                        throw new Error('SmartCaptcha not initialized after ' + maxAttempts + ' attempts');
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Очистка капчи формы
                 */
                function cleanupFormCaptcha(form) {
                    const formId = form.getAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.formId);
                    const container = form.querySelector(CONFIG.selectors.captchaContainer);

                    if (container) {
                        container.remove();
                    }

                    // Удаляем старый errorDiv
                    const errorDiv = form.querySelector('.smartcaptcha-error');
                    if (errorDiv) {
                        errorDiv.remove();
                    }

                    if (formId && window.smartCaptchaInstances.has(formId)) {
                        const instanceId = window.smartCaptchaInstances.get(formId);
                        if (window.smartCaptcha?.destroy) {
                            try {
                                window.smartCaptcha.destroy(instanceId);
                            } catch (e) {}
                        }
                        window.smartCaptchaInstances.delete(formId);
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Сброс капчи для повторной отправки формы
                 */
                function resetCaptcha(form) {
                    const formId = form.getAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.formId);
                    if (formId && window.smartCaptchaInstances.has(formId)) {
                        const instanceId = window.smartCaptchaInstances.get(formId);
                        if (window.smartCaptcha?.reset) {
                            try {
                                window.smartCaptcha.reset(instanceId);
                            } catch (e) {}
                        }
                    }
                    // Очищаем токен
                    const tokenInput = form.querySelector(CONFIG.selectors.smartToken);
                    if (tokenInput) {
                        tokenInput.value = '';
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Снятие класса загрузки
                 */
                function removeLoadingState(form) {
                    form.classList.remove('is-loading');
                    let parent = form.parentElement;
                    while (parent) {
                        parent.classList.remove('is-loading');
                        parent = parent.parentElement;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Инициализация капчи для формы
                 */
                async function initFormCaptcha(form) {
                    try {
                        if (form.hasAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.initialized) || isExcluded(form)) {
                            return;
                        }

                        cleanupFormCaptcha(form);
                        await loadScript();
                        await waitForSmartCaptcha();

                        // Ищем кнопку отправки
                        let submitBtn = form.querySelector(CONFIG.selectors.submitButton);
                        if (!submitBtn) {
                            submitBtn = form.querySelector('button, input[type="button"]');
                        }

                        const insertTarget = submitBtn?.parentNode || form;
                        const insertBefore = submitBtn || null;

                        const formId = 'captcha_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
                        form.setAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.formId, formId);

                        // Создаём контейнер для ошибки
                        const errorDiv = document.createElement('div');
                        errorDiv.className = 'smartcaptcha-error';
                        errorDiv.style.cssText = 'color:#d32f2f;font-size:14px;margin:0 0 15px;display:none;';
                        form.insertBefore(errorDiv, form.firstChild);

                        // Создаём контейнер для капчи
                        const container = document.createElement('div');
                        container.className = CONFIG.classes.captchaContainer;
                        container.innerHTML = captchaHtml.replace('data-form-id=""', 'data-form-id="' + formId + '"');

                        if (insertBefore) {
                            insertTarget.insertBefore(container, insertBefore);
                        } else {
                            insertTarget.appendChild(container);
                        }

                        const captchaElement = container.firstElementChild;
                        if (!captchaElement) {
                            return;
                        }

                        const isInvisible = captchaElement.hasAttribute('data-invisible');

                        if (isInvisible) {
                            container.style.display = 'none';
                        }

                        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, CONFIG.timing.initDelay));

                        // Флаги состояния для невидимой капчи
                        let captchaExecuting = false;
                        let tokenReceived = false;

                        const captchaConfig = {
                            sitekey: captchaElement.getAttribute('data-sitekey'),
                            invisible: isInvisible,
                            test: captchaElement.getAttribute('data-test') === 'true',
                            hideShield: captchaElement.hasAttribute('data-hide-shield'),
                            shieldPosition: captchaElement.getAttribute('data-shield-position'),
                            callback: (token) => {
                                errorDiv.style.display = 'none';
                                if (isInvisible && token) {
                                    tokenReceived = true;
                                    captchaExecuting = false;
                                    // Отправляем форму нативно
                                    form.requestSubmit ? form.requestSubmit(submitBtn) : form.submit();
                                }
                            }
                        };

                        const captchaInstance = window.smartCaptcha.render(captchaElement, captchaConfig);
                        window.smartCaptchaInstances.set(formId, captchaInstance);

                        if (isInvisible) {
                            // Для невидимой капчи — перехватываем submit формы
                            const handleInvisibleSubmit = function(e) {
                                // Если токен уже получен — пропускаем
                                if (tokenReceived) {
                                    tokenReceived = false;
                                    return;
                                }

                                const token = form.querySelector(CONFIG.selectors.smartToken);
                                if (!token || !token.value) {
                                    e.preventDefault();
                                    e.stopImmediatePropagation();

                                    if (!captchaExecuting) {
                                        captchaExecuting = true;
                                        removeLoadingState(form);
                                        window.smartCaptcha.execute(captchaInstance);
                                    }
                                    return false;
                                }
                                // Токен есть, сбрасываем после отправки для повторного использования
                                setTimeout(() => resetCaptcha(form), 1000);
                            };

                            // Перехватываем на фазе capture
                            form.addEventListener('submit', handleInvisibleSubmit, { capture: true });

                            // Перехватываем клик для форм с JS-отправкой
                            if (submitBtn) {
                                submitBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                                    if (tokenReceived) return;

                                    const token = form.querySelector(CONFIG.selectors.smartToken);
                                    if (!token || !token.value) {
                                        e.preventDefault();
                                        e.stopImmediatePropagation();

                                        if (!captchaExecuting) {
                                            captchaExecuting = true;
                                            removeLoadingState(form);
                                            window.smartCaptcha.execute(captchaInstance);
                                        }
                                    }
                                }, { capture: true });
                            }
                        } else {
                            // Для видимой капчи — перехватываем submit формы
                            form.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
                                const token = form.querySelector(CONFIG.selectors.smartToken);
                                if (!token || !token.value) {
                                    e.preventDefault();
                                    e.stopImmediatePropagation();

                                    errorDiv.textContent = CONFIG.messages.captchaRequired;
                                    errorDiv.style.display = 'block';
                                    removeLoadingState(form);
                                } else {
                                    errorDiv.style.display = 'none';
                                    // Сбрасываем после отправки
                                    setTimeout(() => resetCaptcha(form), 1000);
                                }
                            }, { capture: true });
                        }

                        form.setAttribute(CONFIG.attributes.initialized, 'true');

                    } catch (error) {
                        console.error('[SmartCaptcha] Init error:', error);
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Инициализация всех форм
                 */
                function initAllForms() {
                    const forms = findForms();
                    forms.forEach(form => initFormCaptcha(form));
                }

                // Инициализация при загрузке
                if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initAllForms);
                } else {
                    initAllForms();
                }

                // Наблюдатель за новыми формами (для SPA и динамического контента)
                const observer = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
                    const newForms = new Set();

                    mutations.forEach((mutation) => {
                        mutation.addedNodes.forEach((node) => {
                            if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return;

                            try {
                                if (node.matches && node.matches(CONFIG.selectors.form)) {
                                    newForms.add(node);
                                } else if (node.querySelectorAll) {
                                    node.querySelectorAll(CONFIG.selectors.form).forEach(f => newForms.add(f));
                                }
                            } catch (e) {}
                        });
                    });

                    if (newForms.size > 0) {
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            newForms.forEach(form => initFormCaptcha(form));
                        }, CONFIG.timing.domObserverDelay);
                    }
                });

                observer.observe(document.body, {
                    childList: true,
                    subtree: true
                });

                // Публичный API для ручного управления
                window.SmartCaptchaModule = {
                    init: initAllForms,
                    initForm: initFormCaptcha,
                    isExcluded: isExcluded,
                    reset: resetCaptcha,
                    cleanup: cleanupFormCaptcha,
                    getInstances: () => window.smartCaptchaInstances,
                    getConfig: () => CONFIG
                };
            })();
        </script>
</body>
    </html>
